# Open-Gravity living theory/formula status registry

Last verified: 2026-09-01  
Role: canonical, update-in-place status ledger  
Repository root: `C:\Users\henry\Documents\Codex\2026-08-21\Invariant-main-integration`  
Scope: every evidenced Open-Gravity formula family in this workspace: the nine active lanes, GP01, TWELL-400 v2 final-v3, the quantum ontology/AST atlas, registered conventional comparators, and the real-scored 72-item roadmap. Unrelated Invariant/math projects are excluded. Pure prose ideas without an executable or registered formula are listed only through their containing lane.

## Reading rules

- `empirical status` means response evidence only. Theory compilation, source acquisition, and synthetic injections are not empirical support.
- `source-blocked` means the registered real-data test could not legally or technically reach response scoring; it is not a rejection.
- `scoped reject` applies only to the exact registered representation and dataset, never automatically to the whole idea class.
- `pending/current` means an active package has no final audited scientific result yet.
- Paths are repository-relative unless explicitly absolute. Immutable JSON receipts control if this prose ever conflicts with them.
- No sealed confirmation/independent response payload or raw FITS/HDF/TSV/CSV value was opened to compile this registry.

### Source-only drivers

| Driver | Source variable that may shape the state |
|---|---|
| D01_ACC | baryonic acceleration |
| D02_POT | absolute baryonic potential with declared zero/boundary |
| D03_RAD | radius |
| D04_RHO | 3D baryonic density |
| D05_SIG | surface density |
| D06_SLOPE | negative logarithmic profile slope |
| D07_TIDE | Hessian/tidal contraction |
| D08_BAL | support-balance proxy |
| D09_FLAT | flattening |
| D10_MULT | multipole structure |
| D11_EDGE | signed distance from baryonic edge |
| D12_ENV | environment |
| D13_GASF | gas fraction |
| D14_ION | ionization state |
| D15_COOL | cooling/dynamical timescale ratio |
| D16_NTH | nonthermal fraction |
| D17_AGE | age/dynamical ratio |
| D18_RELAX | relaxation state |
| D19_COH | coherence |
| D20_EPOCH | source epoch |

### Exact 400-card appendix

All 400 rows are now bound to the frozen source-shaped replay: 110 formulas are executable on the audited X-COP spherical-static source ABI and 290 are source-blocked there; all 400 are incompatible with the current PHANGS, Solar, lens and void feature ABIs. The replay is synthetic-only with zero real scores. ## Maintenance rule

Add or change a row only when an immutable config/card/receipt/report exists. Preserve old failures as scoped counterevidence. Never convert source availability, compilation, synthetic recovery, or an audit into empirical support. Keep Lane 2 v6 at its independently reproduced pre-response optimizer failure and Lane 7 v5 independently blocked. Lane 9's one authorized v6 run is complete but unscored: it retained a geometry-invalid null/permutation failure. Never promote decoded development rows, executor/parser audits, or that failed null into scientific evidence.
